3:T907f,---
title: ProFields
description: Standalone field primitives for forms — Input, Password, Textarea, Digit, Select, DatePicker, Upload, Captcha, ArrayField, ObjectField, and more.
---

## Installation

<CodeTabs>

<TabsList>
  <TabsTrigger value="cli">CLI</TabsTrigger>
  <TabsTrigger value="manual">Manual</TabsTrigger>
</TabsList>

<TabsContent value="cli">

```bash
npx @draco-china/shadcn-pro@latest add pro-fields
```

</TabsContent>

<TabsContent value="manual">

<Steps className="mb-0 pt-2">

<Step>Install the following dependencies:</Step>

```bash
bun add @dnd-kit/core @dnd-kit/sortable @dnd-kit/utilities class-variance-authority date-fns lucide-react radix-ui react-day-picker
```

<Step>No additional shadcn/ui components are required.</Step>

<Step>
  Copy and paste the component files into your project under
  `components/pro/base/fields/`.
</Step>

Manual installs include these field files:

- `components/pro/base/fields/shared/field.tsx`
- `components/pro/base/fields/shared/classes.ts`
- `components/pro/base/fields/shared/calendar.tsx`
- `components/pro/base/fields/shared/calendar-class-names.ts`
- `components/pro/base/fields/shared/calendar-day-button.tsx`
- `components/pro/base/fields/shared/change-event.ts`
- `components/pro/base/fields/shared/popover.tsx`
- `components/pro/base/fields/shared/select-classes.ts`
- `components/pro/base/fields/shared/select-content.tsx`
- `components/pro/base/fields/shared/select-item.tsx`
- `components/pro/base/fields/shared/select.tsx`
- `components/pro/base/fields/shared/trigger.tsx`
- `components/pro/base/fields/input/index.tsx`
- `components/pro/base/fields/input/affix.tsx`
- `components/pro/base/fields/input/affix-types.ts`
- `components/pro/base/fields/input/classes.ts`
- `components/pro/base/fields/input/clear.ts`
- `components/pro/base/fields/input/types.ts`
- `components/pro/base/fields/input/affix-utils.ts`
- `components/pro/base/fields/input/affix-value.ts`
- `components/pro/base/fields/input/affix-select.tsx`
- `components/pro/base/fields/input/use-affix-selection.ts`
- `components/pro/base/fields/input/use-input-affix.ts`
- `components/pro/base/fields/input/use-input-value.ts`
- `components/pro/base/fields/password/index.tsx`
- `components/pro/base/fields/password/types.ts`
- `components/pro/base/fields/textarea/index.tsx`
- `components/pro/base/fields/textarea/classes.ts`
- `components/pro/base/fields/textarea/types.ts`
- `components/pro/base/fields/select/classes.ts`
- `components/pro/base/fields/select/index.tsx`
- `components/pro/base/fields/select/multi-select.tsx`
- `components/pro/base/fields/select/multi-trigger.tsx`
- `components/pro/base/fields/select/single-select.tsx`
- `components/pro/base/fields/select/single-trigger.tsx`

- `components/pro/base/fields/select/command-options.tsx`

## Usage

Every field is a plain React/shadcn component. Simple fields preserve the underlying shadcn/ui prop surface where practical; shadcn-pro adds only small behaviors such as `allowClear`, prefix/suffix slots, options mapping, and composed field layouts. Formily adapters live under `pro-form/formily-fields`, so schema-driven forms can use the same field primitives without making the base fields depend on Formily.

```tsx
import { Input } from "@/components/pro/base/fields/input"

export default function Example() {
  return <Input placeholder="Enter username" />
}
```

For schema-driven forms, install `pro-form` and import the Formily adapters from `pro-form/formily-fields`.

```tsx
import { createForm } from "@formily/core"
import { Field, FormProvider } from "@formily/react"

import { FormItem } from "@/components/pro/form/form-item"
import { FormilyInput } from "@/components/pro/form/formily-fields"

const form = createForm()

export default function Example() {
  return (
    <FormProvider form={form}>
      <Field
        name="username"
        title="Username"
        decorator={[FormItem]}
        component={[FormilyInput, { placeholder: "Enter username" }]}
      />
    </FormProvider>
  )
}
```

---

## FacetedFilter

A Popover + Checkbox filter component designed for faceted search — auto-used by `ProTable` when `meta.filter.options` is set on a column, and available standalone for forms or custom toolbars. Supports single-select and multi-select behavior with `multiple`.

<ComponentPreview
  name="pro-fields-faceted-filter-demo"
  previewClassName="min-h-[260px] items-start p-6"
  align="start"
/>

### API Reference

| Prop          | Type                                | Default     | Description                                              |
| ------------- | ----------------------------------- | ----------- | -------------------------------------------------------- |
| `value`       | `string \| string[]`                | —           | Controlled selected value(s)                             |
| `onChange`    | `(value: string \| string[] \| undefined) => void` | — | Change handler                              |
| `options`     | `{ label: string; value: string; icon?: ComponentType }[]` | `[]` | Filter options                      |
| `placeholder` | `string`                            | `'Filter...'` | Button label / search placeholder                      |
| `multiple`    | `boolean`                           | `false`     | Return `string[]` values when true; return a single `string` when false |
| `facets`      | `Map<string, number>`               | —           | Optional count map, usually from TanStack Table's `getFacetedUniqueValues()`. |
| `className`   | `string`                            | —           | Additional CSS classes                                   |

```tsx
import { FacetedFilter } from "@/components/pro/base/filter/faceted-filter"

<FacetedFilter
  placeholder="Status"
  multiple
  options={[
    { label: "Active", value: "active" },
    { label: "Inactive", value: "inactive" },
  ]}
  value={selected}
  onChange={setSelected}
/>
```

## Upload

<ComponentPreview
  name="pro-fields-upload-demo"
  previewClassName="min-h-[320px] items-start p-6"
  align="start"
/>

### API Reference

| Prop          | Type                            | Default                     | Description          |
| ------------- | ------------------------------- | --------------------------- | -------------------- |
| `value`       | `UploadFile[]`                  | —                           | Controlled file list |
| `onChange`    | `(files: UploadFile[]) => void` | —                           | Change handler       |
| `upload` | `(files, context) => UploadResult \| UploadResult[] \| Promise<...>` | — | Custom upload behavior for the selected files. Return URL strings, `UploadFile` patches, `false`/`null` to skip, or nothing to use the default object URL. |
| `accept`      | `string`                        | —                           | Accepted MIME types  |
| `multiple`    | `boolean`                       | `true`                      | Allow multiple files |
| `maxCount`    | `number`                        | —                           | Maximum file count   |
| `disabled`    | `boolean`                       | `false`                     | Disable interaction  |
| `placeholder` | `string`                        | `'Click or drag to upload'` | Trigger label or URL input placeholder |
| `presentation` | `'dropzone' \| 'compact' \| 'input'` | `'dropzone'` | Trigger layout. Every presentation supports click and drag upload; `input` also supports URL entry. |
| `preview` | `boolean` | `true` in input presentation | Show the prefix preview in `input` presentation; set `false` to hide it. |
| `fileList` | `boolean` | `true` in dropzone presentation | Show the selected file list below the trigger. |

```ts
interface UploadFile {
  uid: string
  name: string
  url?: string
  status?: "uploading" | "done" | "error"
  size?: number
}

type UploadResult = string | Partial<UploadFile> | null | false | undefined

interface UploadContext {
  maxCount?: number
  multiple: boolean
}
```

```tsx
<Upload
  presentation="input"
  multiple={false}
  upload={async ([file]) => {
    const url = await uploadAvatar(file)
    return { url, name: file.name, status: "done" }
  }}
/>
```

```tsx
<Upload
  multiple
  upload={async (files) => {
    const urls = await uploadAttachments(files)
    return urls.map((url, index) => ({ url, name: files[index].name }))
  }}
/>
```

## Captcha

<ComponentPreview
  name="pro-fields-captcha-demo"
  previewClassName="min-h-[260px] items-start p-6"
  align="start"
/>

### API Reference

| Prop              | Type          | Default           | Description                                                                                    |
| ----------------- | ------------- | ----------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `value`           | `string`      | —                 | Controlled input value                                                                         |
| `onChange`        | `(e) => void` | —                 | Input change handler                                                                           |
| `onSend`          | `() => void \| Promise<void>`  | —       | Called before the SMS send-code countdown starts.                                              |
| `buttonText`      | `string`      | `'Get code'`      | Text for the trailing send-code button.                                                        |
| `countdown`       | `number`      | `60000`           | Countdown duration in milliseconds after clicking the trailing send-code button. Set `0` to disable countdown. |
| `prefix`          | `ReactNode \| object` | —          | Same prefix slot as `Input`, including compact select affix config.                            |
| `suffix`          | `ReactNode \| object` | —          | Same suffix slot as `Input`; the send-code button is appended after it.                        |
| `allowClear`      | `boolean`     | `true`            | Show the shared `Input` clear button when the field has a value.                               |
| `onClear`         | `() => void`  | —                 | Called when the shared clear button is clicked.                                                |
| `placeholder`     | `string`      | `'Enter captcha'` | Input placeholder                                                                              |
| `disabled`        | `boolean`     | `false`           | Disable interaction                                                                            |
| `inputClassName`  | `string`      | —                 | Additional className for the input.                                                            |
| `buttonClassName` | `string`      | —                 | Additional className for the trailing send-code button.                                        |
| `className`       | `string`      | —                 | Additional CSS classes                                                                         |

`VisualCaptcha` is the visual captcha variant. Choose it explicitly when the suffix should render an image or text captcha instead of the SMS send-code button.

| Prop              | Type          | Default           | Description                                                                                    |
| ----------------- | ------------- | ----------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `value`           | `string`      | —                 | Controlled input value                                                                         |
| `onChange`        | `(e) => void` | —                 | Input change handler                                                                           |
| `onRefresh`       | `() => void \| Promise<void>`  | —       | Called when the visual captcha refresh button is clicked.                                      |
| `captchaUrl`      | `string`      | —                 | Image URL for image captcha.                                                                   |
| `captchaText`     | `string`      | —                 | Text captcha fallback when no image URL is provided.                                           |
| `prefix`          | `ReactNode \| object` | —          | Same prefix slot as `Input`, including compact select affix config.                            |
| `suffix`          | `ReactNode \| object` | —          | Same suffix slot as `Input`; the visual captcha is appended after it.                          |
| `allowClear`      | `boolean`     | `true`            | Show the shared `Input` clear button when the field has a value.                               |
| `onClear`         | `() => void`  | —                 | Called when the shared clear button is clicked.                                                |
| `placeholder`     | `string`      | `'Enter captcha'` | Input placeholder                                                                              |
| `disabled`        | `boolean`     | `false`           | Disable interaction                                                                            |
| `inputClassName`  | `string`      | —                 | Additional className for the input.                                                            |
| `className`       | `string`      | —                 | Additional CSS classes                                                                         |

  decorator={[FormItem]}

### Select

<ComponentPreview
  name="pro-fields-select-demo"
  previewClassName="min-h-[160px] items-start p-6"
  align="start"
/>

```tsx
<Field
  name="role"
  title="Role"
  decorator={[FormItem]}
  component={[
    FormilySelect,
    {
      allowClear: true,
      searchable: true,
      options: [
        {
          label: "Admin",
          value: "admin",
          description: "Full access to team settings.",
        },
        {
          label: "User",
          value: "user",
          description: "Can view and update assigned work.",
        },
      ],
    },
  ]}
/>
```

Most clearable fields enable `allowClear` by default. `Select` keeps `allowClear` disabled by default and only shows the clear button when you enable it explicitly. Clearing `Select` sends `undefined` so Formily can return to an empty value.

`Select`, `Radio`, and grouped `Checkbox` options support `description` for consistent option help text. `Select` also supports `searchable` and `multiple`; multiple mode uses a `string[]` value.

```tsx
<Field
  name="roles"
  title="Roles"
  decorator={[FormItem]}
  component={[
    FormilySelect,
    {
      multiple: true,
      searchable: true,
      placeholder: "Select roles",
      options: [
        { label: "Admin", value: "admin", description: "Full access." },
        { label: "Editor", value: "editor", description: "Can edit content." },
      ],
    },
  ]}
/>
```

### Checkbox

<ComponentPreview
  name="pro-fields-checkbox-demo"
  previewClassName="min-h-[140px] items-start p-6"
  align="start"
/>

```tsx
<Field
  name="agree"
  title="Agree to terms"
  decorator={[FormItem]}
  component={[FormilyCheckbox]}
/>
```

Use `options` for checkbox group mode. In group mode the value is `string[]`; without `options`, Checkbox uses boolean switch semantics.

```tsx
<Field
  name="roles"
  title="Roles"
  decorator={[FormItem]}
  component={[
    FormilyCheckbox,
    {
      options: [
        {
          label: "Admin",
          value: "admin",
          description: "Can manage workspace settings.",
        },
        {
          label: "Editor",
          value: "editor",
          description: "Can edit shared content.",
        },
      ],
    },
  ]}
/>
```

```tsx
<Field
  name="avatarUrl"
  title="Avatar URL"
  decorator={[FormItem]}
  component={[
    FormilyUpload,
    {
      accept: "image/*",
      presentation: "input",
      multiple: false,
      maxCount: 1,
      placeholder: "Paste image URL or upload",
    },
  ]}
/>
```

Input mode is intended for single-file inputs, such as avatar or image URL fields. The value remains an `UploadFile[]`, so pasted URLs and locally selected files use the same data shape.

### ArrayField

Drag-to-reorder list with built-in state management. Supports controlled (`value` + `onChange`) and uncontrolled (`defaultValue`) usage.

<ComponentPreview
  name="pro-fields-array-field-demo"
  previewClassName="min-h-[280px] items-start p-6"
  align="start"
/>

| Prop | Type | Default | Description |
|------|------|---------|-------------|
| `value` | `TItem[]` | — | Controlled array value |
| `onChange` | `(value: TItem[]) => void` | — | Called on any change |
| `defaultValue` | `TItem[]` | `[]` | Initial value (uncontrolled) |
| `newItem` | `() => TItem` | — | Factory for blank items |
| `renderItem` | `(item, index, helpers) => ReactNode` | — | Render each item. `helpers.update` accepts a full replacement value or an updater function; `helpers.remove` removes the row. |
| `addText` | `ReactNode` | `'Add item'` | Add button label |
| `max` | `number` | — | Hide Add button when reached |
| `min` | `number` | `0` | Hide Remove button when at minimum |
| `disabled` | `boolean` | `false` | Disables all controls |

### ObjectField

Lightweight fieldset with title, description, action slot, collapsible support, and two visual variants.

<ComponentPreview
  name="pro-fields-object-field-demo"
  previewClassName="min-h-[260px] items-start p-6"
  align="start"
/>

| Prop | Type | Default | Description |
|------|------|---------|-------------|
| `title` | `ReactNode` | — | Section heading |
| `description` | `ReactNode` | — | Helper text below title |
| `action` | `ReactNode` | — | Element rendered to the right of the title |
| `variant` | `'separated' \| 'bordered' \| 'none'` | `'separated'` | Visual style |
| `collapsible` | `boolean` | `false` | Whether section can be toggled |
| `defaultOpen` | `boolean` | `true` | Initial open state when collapsible |

  { label: 'Backlog', value: 'backlog' },

  ['done', 24],
])

const priorityFacets = new Map([
  ['low', 6],
  ['medium', 18],
  ['high', 5],
])

export function ProFieldsFacetedFilterDemo() {
  const [status, setStatus] = useState<string[]>(['in-progress'])
  const [priority, setPriority] = useState<string | undefined>('high')

  return (
    <div className="w-full max-w-md space-y-6 p-4">
      <div className="space-y-2">
        <Label className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Multi-select</Label>
        <FacetedFilter
          placeholder="Status"
          multiple
          value={status}
          options={statusOptions}
          facets={statusFacets}
          onChange={(nextValue) => {
            setStatus(Array.isArray(nextValue) ? nextValue : nextValue ? [nextValue] : [])
          }}
        />
      </div>
      <div className="space-y-2">
        <Label className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Single-select</Label>
        <FacetedFilter
          placeholder="Priority"
          value={priority}
          options={priorityOptions}
          facets={priorityFacets}
          onChange={(nextValue) => {
            setPriority(typeof nextValue === 'string' ? nextValue : undefined)
          }}
        />
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

164:T4d1,"use client"

import { createForm } from "@formily/core"
import { Field, FormProvider } from "@formily/react"

import { FormItem } from "@/components/pro/form/form-item"
import { FormilySlider } from "@/components/pro/form/formily-fields"

const form = createForm()

export function ProFieldsSliderDemo() {
  return (
    <FormProvider form={form}>
      <div className="w-full max-w-sm space-y-6 p-4">
        <Field
          name="volume"
          title="Volume"
          initialValue={40}
          decorator={[FormItem]}
          component={[
            FormilySlider,
            { min: 0, max: 100, step: 1, showValue: true },
          ]}
        />
        <Field
          name="opacity"
          title="Opacity"
          initialValue={70}
          decorator={[FormItem]}
          component={[
            FormilySlider,
            { min: 0, max: 100, step: 10, showValue: true },
          ]}
        />
        <Field
          name="disabled"
          title="Disabled"
          initialValue={30}
          decorator={[FormItem]}
          component={[
            FormilySlider,
            { min: 0, max: 100, disabled: true, showValue: true },
          ]}
        />
      </div>
    </FormProvider>
  )
}

173:T541,"use client"

import { createForm } from "@formily/core"
import { Field, FormProvider } from "@formily/react"

import { FormItem } from "@/components/pro/form/form-item"
import { FormilySegmented } from "@/components/pro/form/formily-fields"

const form = createForm()

export function ProFieldsSegmentedDemo() {
  return (
    <FormProvider form={form}>
      <div className="w-full max-w-sm space-y-6 p-4">
        <Field
          name="view"
          title="View"
          initialValue="list"
          decorator={[FormItem]}
          component={[
            FormilySegmented,
            {
              options: [
                { label: "List", value: "list" },
                { label: "Board", value: "board" },
                { label: "Calendar", value: "calendar" },
              ],
            },
          ]}
        />
        <Field
          name="size"
          title="Size"
          initialValue="md"
          decorator={[FormItem]}
          component={[
            FormilySegmented,
            {
              options: [
                { label: "S", value: "sm" },
                { label: "M", value: "md" },
                { label: "L", value: "lg" },
                { label: "XL", value: "xl", disabled: true },
              ],
            },
          ]}
        />
      </div>
    </FormProvider>
  )
}

179:T6ca,"use client"

import { createForm } from "@formily/core"
import { Field, FormProvider } from "@formily/react"

import { FormItem } from "@/components/pro/form/form-item"
import { FormilyCascader } from "@/components/pro/form/formily-fields"

const regionOptions = [
  {
    label: "North America",
    value: "na",
    children: [
      {
        label: "United States",
        value: "us",
        children: [
          { label: "California", value: "ca" },
          { label: "New York", value: "ny" },
          { label: "Texas", value: "tx" },
        ],
      },
      {
        label: "Canada",
        value: "ca-country",
        children: [
          { label: "Ontario", value: "on" },
          { label: "British Columbia", value: "bc" },
        ],
      },
    ],
  },
  {
    label: "Asia",
    value: "asia",
    children: [
      {
        label: "China",
        value: "cn",
        children: [
          { label: "Beijing", value: "bj" },
          { label: "Shanghai", value: "sh" },
        ],
      },
      {
        label: "Japan",
        value: "jp",
        children: [
          { label: "Tokyo", value: "tk" },
          { label: "Osaka", value: "os" },
        ],
      },
    ],
  },
]

const form = createForm()

export function ProFieldsCascaderDemo() {
  return (
    <FormProvider form={form}>
      <div className="w-full max-w-sm space-y-6 p-4">
        <Field
          name="region"
          title="Region"
          decorator={[FormItem]}
          component={[
            FormilyCascader,
            {
              options: regionOptions,
              placeholder: "Select region",
              allowClear: true,
            },
          ]}
        />
      </div>
    </FormProvider>
  )
}

180:T63e,"use client"

import { createForm } from "@formily/core"
import { Field, FormProvider } from "@formily/react"

import { FormItem } from "@/components/pro/form/form-item"
import { FormilyTreeSelect } from "@/components/pro/form/formily-fields"

const deptOptions = [
  {
    label: "Engineering",
    value: "eng",
    children: [
      { label: "Frontend", value: "frontend" },
      { label: "Backend", value: "backend" },
      { label: "DevOps", value: "devops" },
    ],
  },
  {
    label: "Design",
    value: "design",
    children: [
      { label: "UI/UX", value: "uiux" },
      { label: "Brand", value: "brand" },
    ],
  },
  {
    label: "Marketing",
    value: "marketing",
  },
]

const form = createForm()

export function ProFieldsTreeSelectDemo() {
  return (
    <FormProvider form={form}>
      <div className="w-full max-w-sm space-y-6 p-4">
        <Field
          name="department"
          title="Department (single)"
          decorator={[FormItem]}
          component={[
            FormilyTreeSelect,
            {
              options: deptOptions,
              placeholder: "Select department",
              allowClear: true,
            },
          ]}
        />
        <Field
          name="teams"
          title="Teams (multi-select)"
          decorator={[FormItem]}
          component={[
            FormilyTreeSelect,
            {
              options: deptOptions,
              placeholder: "Select teams",
              multiple: true,
              allowClear: true,
            },
          ]}
        />
      </div>
    </FormProvider>
  )
}

import { createForm } from "@formily/core"
import { Field, FormProvider } from "@formily/react"

import { FormItem } from "@/components/pro/form/form-item"
import { FormilyUpload } from "@/components/pro/form/formily-fields"

const form = createForm()

export function ProFieldsUploadDemo() {
  return (
    <FormProvider form={form}>
      <div className="w-full max-w-sm space-y-6 p-4">
        <Field
          name="avatar"
          title="Avatar"
          decorator={[FormItem]}
          component={[
            FormilyUpload,
            {
              accept: "image/*",
              multiple: false,
              maxCount: 1,
              placeholder: "Click to upload image",
            },
          ]}
        />
        <Field
          name="contract"
          title="Contract"
          decorator={[FormItem]}
          component={[
            FormilyUpload,
            {
              accept: ".pdf,.doc,.docx",
              multiple: false,
              maxCount: 1,
              placeholder: "Upload contract",
              presentation: "compact",
            },
          ]}
        />
        <Field
          name="avatarUrl"
          title="Avatar URL"
          decorator={[FormItem]}
          component={[
            FormilyUpload,
            {
              accept: "image/*",
              multiple: false,
              maxCount: 1,
              placeholder: "Paste image URL or upload",
              presentation: "input",
            },
          ]}
        />
        <Field
          name="documents"
          title="Documents"
          decorator={[FormItem]}
          component={[
            FormilyUpload,
            {
              accept: ".pdf,.doc,.docx",
              multiple: true,
              maxCount: 5,
              placeholder: "Upload up to 5 documents",
            },
          ]}
        />
      </div>
    </FormProvider>
  )
}

1d9:T48c,"use client"

import { createForm } from "@formily/core"
import { Field, FormProvider } from "@formily/react"

import { FormItem } from "@/components/pro/form/form-item"
import { FormilyMoney } from "@/components/pro/form/formily-fields"

const form = createForm()

export function ProFieldsMoneyDemo() {
  return (
    <FormProvider form={form}>
      <div className="w-full max-w-sm space-y-6 p-4">
        <Field
          name="price"
          title="Price (USD)"
          decorator={[FormItem]}
          component={[
            FormilyMoney,
            { currency: "$", precision: 2, placeholder: "0.00" },
          ]}
        />
        <Field
          name="budget"
          title="Budget (EUR)"
          decorator={[FormItem]}
          component={[
            FormilyMoney,
            { currency: "€", precision: 2, placeholder: "0.00" },
          ]}
        />
        <Field
          name="amount"
          title="Amount (JPY)"
          decorator={[FormItem]}
          component={[
            FormilyMoney,
            { currency: "¥", precision: 0, placeholder: "0" },
          ]}
        />
      </div>
    </FormProvider>
  )
}

1f9:T48c,"use client"

import { createForm } from "@formily/core"
import { Field, FormProvider } from "@formily/react"

import { FormItem } from "@/components/pro/form/form-item"
import { FormilyMoney } from "@/components/pro/form/formily-fields"

const form = createForm()

export function ProFieldsMoneyDemo() {
  return (
    <FormProvider form={form}>
      <div className="w-full max-w-sm space-y-6 p-4">
        <Field
          name="price"
          title="Price (USD)"
          decorator={[FormItem]}
          component={[
            FormilyMoney,
            { currency: "$", precision: 2, placeholder: "0.00" },
          ]}
        />
        <Field
          name="budget"
          title="Budget (EUR)"
          decorator={[FormItem]}
          component={[
            FormilyMoney,
            { currency: "€", precision: 2, placeholder: "0.00" },
          ]}
        />
        <Field
          name="amount"
          title="Amount (JPY)"
          decorator={[FormItem]}
          component={[
            FormilyMoney,
            { currency: "¥", precision: 0, placeholder: "0" },
          ]}
        />
      </div>
    </FormProvider>
  )
}

201:T663,"use client"

import { useState } from "react"

import { Select } from "@/components/pro/base/fields/select"

export function ProFieldsSelectDemo() {
  const [value, setValue] = useState<string | undefined>("admin")
  const [roles, setRoles] = useState<string[]>(["editor"])

  return (
    <div className="grid w-full max-w-sm gap-4 p-4">
      <Select
        value={value}
        onChange={(next) => setValue(typeof next === "string" ? next : undefined)}
        allowClear
        searchable
        options={[
          {
            label: "Admin",
            value: "admin",
            description: "Full access to workspace settings.",
          },
          {
            label: "Editor",
            value: "editor",
            description: "Can create and update content.",
          },
          {
            label: "Viewer",
            value: "viewer",
            description: "Read-only project access.",
          },
        ]}
      />
      <Select
        value={roles}
        onChange={(next) => setRoles(Array.isArray(next) ? next : [])}
        multiple
        searchable
        allowClear
        placeholder="Select roles"
        options={[
          {
            label: "Admin",
            value: "admin",
            description: "Full access to workspace settings.",
          },
          {
            label: "Editor",
            value: "editor",
            description: "Can create and update content.",
          },
          {
            label: "Viewer",
            value: "viewer",
            description: "Read-only project access.",
          },
        ]}
      />
    </div>
  )
}

246:T541,"use client"

import { createForm } from "@formily/core"
import { Field, FormProvider } from "@formily/react"

import { FormItem } from "@/components/pro/form/form-item"
import { FormilySegmented } from "@/components/pro/form/formily-fields"

const form = createForm()

export function ProFieldsSegmentedDemo() {
  return (
    <FormProvider form={form}>
      <div className="w-full max-w-sm space-y-6 p-4">
        <Field
          name="view"
          title="View"
          initialValue="list"
          decorator={[FormItem]}
          component={[
            FormilySegmented,
            {
              options: [
                { label: "List", value: "list" },
                { label: "Board", value: "board" },
                { label: "Calendar", value: "calendar" },
              ],
            },
          ]}
        />
        <Field
          name="size"
          title="Size"
          initialValue="md"
          decorator={[FormItem]}
          component={[
            FormilySegmented,
            {
              options: [
                { label: "S", value: "sm" },
                { label: "M", value: "md" },
                { label: "L", value: "lg" },
                { label: "XL", value: "xl", disabled: true },
              ],
            },
          ]}
        />
      </div>
    </FormProvider>
  )
}

253:T4d1,"use client"

import { createForm } from "@formily/core"
import { Field, FormProvider } from "@formily/react"

import { FormItem } from "@/components/pro/form/form-item"
import { FormilySlider } from "@/components/pro/form/formily-fields"

const form = createForm()

export function ProFieldsSliderDemo() {
  return (
    <FormProvider form={form}>
      <div className="w-full max-w-sm space-y-6 p-4">
        <Field
          name="volume"
          title="Volume"
          initialValue={40}
          decorator={[FormItem]}
          component={[
            FormilySlider,
            { min: 0, max: 100, step: 1, showValue: true },
          ]}
        />
        <Field
          name="opacity"
          title="Opacity"
          initialValue={70}
          decorator={[FormItem]}
          component={[
            FormilySlider,
            { min: 0, max: 100, step: 10, showValue: true },
          ]}
        />
        <Field
          name="disabled"
          title="Disabled"
          initialValue={30}
          decorator={[FormItem]}
          component={[
            FormilySlider,
            { min: 0, max: 100, disabled: true, showValue: true },
          ]}
        />
      </div>
    </FormProvider>
  )
}

import { createForm } from "@formily/core"
import { Field, FormProvider } from "@formily/react"

import { FormItem } from "@/components/pro/form/form-item"
import { FormilyCascader } from "@/components/pro/form/formily-fields"

const regionOptions = [
  {
    label: "North America",
    value: "na",
    children: [
      {
        label: "United States",
        value: "us",
        children: [
          { label: "California", value: "ca" },
          { label: "New York", value: "ny" },
          { label: "Texas", value: "tx" },
        ],
      },
      {
        label: "Canada",
        value: "ca-country",
        children: [
          { label: "Ontario", value: "on" },
          { label: "British Columbia", value: "bc" },
        ],
      },
    ],
  },
  {
    label: "Asia",
    value: "asia",
    children: [
      {
        label: "China",
        value: "cn",
        children: [
          { label: "Beijing", value: "bj" },
          { label: "Shanghai", value: "sh" },
        ],
      },
      {
        label: "Japan",
        value: "jp",
        children: [
          { label: "Tokyo", value: "tk" },
          { label: "Osaka", value: "os" },
        ],
      },
    ],
  },
]

const form = createForm()

export function ProFieldsCascaderDemo() {
  return (
    <FormProvider form={form}>
      <div className="w-full max-w-sm space-y-6 p-4">
        <Field
          name="region"
          title="Region"
          decorator={[FormItem]}
          component={[
            FormilyCascader,
            {
              options: regionOptions,
              placeholder: "Select region",
              allowClear: true,
            },
          ]}
        />
      </div>
    </FormProvider>
  )
}

275:T63e,"use client"

import { createForm } from "@formily/core"
import { Field, FormProvider } from "@formily/react"

import { FormItem } from "@/components/pro/form/form-item"
import { FormilyTreeSelect } from "@/components/pro/form/formily-fields"

const deptOptions = [
  {
    label: "Engineering",
    value: "eng",
    children: [
      { label: "Frontend", value: "frontend" },
      { label: "Backend", value: "backend" },
      { label: "DevOps", value: "devops" },
    ],
  },
  {
    label: "Design",
    value: "design",
    children: [
      { label: "UI/UX", value: "uiux" },
      { label: "Brand", value: "brand" },
    ],
  },
  {
    label: "Marketing",
    value: "marketing",
  },
]

const form = createForm()

export function ProFieldsTreeSelectDemo() {
  return (
    <FormProvider form={form}>
      <div className="w-full max-w-sm space-y-6 p-4">
        <Field
          name="department"
          title="Department (single)"
          decorator={[FormItem]}
          component={[
            FormilyTreeSelect,
            {
              options: deptOptions,
              placeholder: "Select department",
              allowClear: true,
            },
          ]}
        />
        <Field
          name="teams"
          title="Teams (multi-select)"
          decorator={[FormItem]}
          component={[
            FormilyTreeSelect,
            {
              options: deptOptions,
              placeholder: "Select teams",
              multiple: true,
              allowClear: true,
            },
          ]}
        />
      </div>
    </FormProvider>
  )
}

282:T783,"use client"

import { createForm } from "@formily/core"
import { Field, FormProvider } from "@formily/react"

import { FormItem } from "@/components/pro/form/form-item"
import { FormilyUpload } from "@/components/pro/form/formily-fields"

const form = createForm()

export function ProFieldsUploadDemo() {
  return (
    <FormProvider form={form}>
      <div className="w-full max-w-sm space-y-6 p-4">
        <Field
          name="avatar"
          title="Avatar"
          decorator={[FormItem]}
          component={[
            FormilyUpload,
            {
              accept: "image/*",
              multiple: false,
              maxCount: 1,
              placeholder: "Click to upload image",
            },
          ]}
        />
        <Field
          name="contract"
          title="Contract"
          decorator={[FormItem]}
          component={[
            FormilyUpload,
            {
              accept: ".pdf,.doc,.docx",
              multiple: false,
              maxCount: 1,
              placeholder: "Upload contract",
              presentation: "compact",
            },
          ]}
        />
        <Field
          name="avatarUrl"
          title="Avatar URL"
          decorator={[FormItem]}
          component={[
            FormilyUpload,
            {
              accept: "image/*",
              multiple: false,
              maxCount: 1,
              placeholder: "Paste image URL or upload",
              presentation: "input",
            },
          ]}
        />
        <Field
          name="documents"
          title="Documents"
          decorator={[FormItem]}
          component={[
            FormilyUpload,
            {
              accept: ".pdf,.doc,.docx",
              multiple: true,
              maxCount: 5,
              placeholder: "Upload up to 5 documents",
            },
          ]}
        />
      </div>
    </FormProvider>
  )
}

28f:T487,"use client"

import { useState } from "react"

import { ArrayField } from "@/components/pro/base/fields/array-field"
import { Input } from "@/components/pro/base/fields/input"

interface Contact {
  name: string
  email: string
}

export function ProFieldsArrayFieldDemo() {
  const [value, setValue] = useState<Contact[]>([
    { name: "Alice Wang", email: "alice@example.com" },
    { name: "Bob Chen", email: "bob@example.com" },
  ])

  return (
    <div className="w-full max-w-md p-4">
      <ArrayField<Contact>
        value={value}
        onChange={setValue}
        newItem={() => ({ name: "", email: "" })}
        addText="Add contact"
        min={1}
        renderItem={(item, _index, { update }) => (
          <div className="space-y-2">
            <Input
              placeholder="Name"
              value={item.name}
              onChange={(e) => update({ ...item, name: e.target.value })}
            />
            <Input
              placeholder="Email"
              value={item.email}
              onChange={(e) => update({ ...item, email: e.target.value })}
            />
          </div>
        )}
      />
    </div>
  )
}

  return (
    <div className="w-full max-w-md space-y-6 p-4">
      {/* Default: separated variant */}

        title="Profile"
        description="Your public display information."
        action={<Button variant="ghost" size="sm" className="h-6 text-xs">Edit</Button>}
      >
        <Input placeholder="Display name" />
        <Select
          placeholder="Department"

      >
        <Input placeholder="Custom domain" />

      </ObjectField>
    </div>
  )
}
